\
#+title: The HTTP route inventory
#+description: Every route the BFF and ores.http answer today, read from the code that registers them, decided against the design's four-way rule, with the published area map and the composed views' obligations.
#+type: knowledge
#+level: cross
#+filetags: :knowledge:architecture:http:
#+created: 2026-10-31
#+updated: 2026-10-31

Every HTTP route either server answers, read from its own registration rather than
typed by hand. The design's four-way rule (a resource, a composed view, a transport
capability, or a rendering concern that is not an API) decides every row, and the
=Kind=, =Area= and =Note= columns state that decision.

* How to regenerate

#+begin_src sh :results verbatim
python3 build/scripts/route_inventory.py --write
#+end_src

The decisions are data in =build/scripts/route_classification.py=: an ordered list
of rules over the method and path, an exception for a route no rule decides, and the
owner, computation and cache policy each composed view owes. A regeneration rewrites
every cell from them, so the table states the decisions and never holds a second copy
of them.

Two sections are derived from the same decisions and appear before the table: the
area map, which is the published namespace of design section 11.2 and the areas this
table publishes beside it, and the composed views, whose owner column names the
component that must answer each one after the port.

* How it is enforced

=python3 build/scripts/route_inventory.py --check= fails when a route answered by
either server is absent from this table, when a row names a route neither server
answers, when two routes share one identity, when a route is unclassified, and when a
decision names no published kind or area, carries no evidence, or calls itself a
composed view without stating its owner, computation and cache policy. It also fails
when a pair in =build/scripts/route_pairing.py= names a route that neither server
answers, and when the coverage section does not match the pairs. The gate runs it as
the =route-inventory= check.

* What it reads, and what it cannot

The BFF declares every route with a literal path beginning =/api/=, so a route is a
call whose first argument is that literal. A path built from a template literal in
these sources is a =Map= lookup and not a route; none registers a route that way
today.

ores.http declares its routes with =router->get/post/put/patch/delete_/head=, and
five of them state a path the code composes from a prefix constant; those rows name
the variable and the line that declares it.

What neither shape covers is a route registered some other way: a generated route
table (K1), or a loop that registers a family. When such a route appears, the answer
is to teach this tool that shape, not to add a row by hand: the row would not be
verified.

* The area map

The published areas (design section 11.2) and the areas this table
publishes beside them, each with the components that answer it today,
its size, and the kinds its routes are decided as. A route that is not
an API publishes no area, so it is counted under =(none)=.

| Area | Owner components | Routes | Kinds |
|------+------------------+--------+-------|
| access | iam, server | 108 | composed view 1, resource 107 |
| agile | agile-routes | 6 | composed view 2, resource 4 |
| analytics | sensitivity-templates | 6 | resource 6 |
| assets | assets, server | 38 | composed view 1, resource 37 |
| auth | audit, iam, server | 57 | resource 52, transport capability 5 |
| changes | core, server | 5 | transport capability 5 |
| grid | grid-app-versions, grid-downloads, grid-jobs, operations | 14 | composed view 3, resource 11 |
| market-data | adapters, connectivity, curves, fixing-series, public-rates, series, subscriptions | 31 | composed view 6, resource 25 |
| operations | core, inbox, operations, server | 7 | composed view 2, resource 3, transport capability 2 |
| qa | qa | 6 | resource 6 |
| reference-data | conventions, counterparties, curve-configuration, records, refdata, server, tenors | 277 | composed view 1, resource 276 |
| reporting | cuts, engine-settings, grid-report-results, grid-report-runs, pricing-engines, report-run-files, reporting | 44 | composed view 12, resource 32 |
| simulation | calibration, market-regime, synthetic | 38 | composed view 3, resource 35 |
| tenancy | iam, server | 66 | composed view 2, resource 64 |
| trading | blotter, book-changes, booking, books, structures, trades | 24 | composed view 2, resource 22 |
| workflow | inbox, server, timeline | 18 | composed view 6, resource 12 |
| (none) | books, classifications, conventions, core, counterparties, oresmd, pricing-engines, records, refdata, reporting, sensitivity-templates, server, synthetic, tenors | 401 | not an API 383, transport capability 18 |

* The composed views

A composed view owes four things, and the fourth is why the owner column
exists: without it the view is the old BFF again. The name is the view's
own; the schema is K1's to generate; the owner is the component that must
answer it after the port, which is not always the one that answers it
today.

Every route here is one a screen calls now, and the =Called by= column says
which client module calls it, so the port can prove a view with the same
request against either server. A view keeps its current route until the
cutover for that reason: moving it to the published namespace is a versioned
change of its own, not part of the port.

| View | Routes today | Called by | Owner | Computation | Cache policy |
|------+--------------+-----------+-------+-------------+--------------|
| a report configuration | GET /api/reporting/configurations/:id | web/src/api/client.ts | ores.reporting | the configuration, the names of its type and parties, its analytics and their names, and its market data bindings, five reads | ETag on the whole view, stale after 60 s |
| an ORE schema's contents | GET /api/reporting/ore-files/:code/schema | web/src/api/client.ts | ores.reporting | the catalogue entry that is a schema with this code, and its stored contents at the engine version asked for, two reads | no cache: the schema is read from the store per request |
| an item's fixing series | GET /api/marketdata/fixing-series | web/src/api/client.ts | ores.marketdata | the market series found by URI, with the providers, feeds, scopes, sessions and suppliers read beside it, then its fixings | ETag on the whole view, stale after 5 min |
| one subject's story | GET /api/timeline | web/src/api/client.ts | ores.workflow | every component's rows for one subject, each source read under its owner's permission and a refused source reported as a gap | ETag on the whole view, stale after 30 s |
| the approval inbox | GET /api/requests | web/src/api/client.ts | ores.workflow | every kind of request this person may decide, gathered into one queue with each kind's subject | ETag on the whole view, stale after 15 s |
| the authorisation queue | GET /api/queue/authorisations | web/src/api/queue.ts | ores.inbox | the request queue for this viewer, each open and answered request joined to the deal it names | no cache: the queue answers what this person may decide now |
| the blotter | GET /api/blotter | web/src/api/blotter.ts | ores.trading | one page of trades with the rail that groups them: books, portfolios, structures, counterparties, trade types, instrument codes and asset classes, eight collection reads before the page | ETag on the whole view, stale after 30 s |
| the book tree | GET /api/books/tree | web/src/api/books.ts | ores.trading | every portfolio and every book, two collection reads woven into the tree | ETag on the whole view, stale after 60 s |
| the bootstrap status | GET /api/bootstrap | web/src/api/client.ts | ores.iam | the IAM account and tenant answer, plus a variability settings read that may fail without failing the answer | ETag on the whole view, stale after 5 s |
| the calibration of an asset class | GET /api/synthetic/calibration/:assetClass | web/src/api/client.ts | ores.synthetic | every feed of the class resolved as a start would, with its candidates and their reasons, under the class policy or the defaults; nothing starts | No cache: a resolution reads the newest fixings each time |
| the connectivity catalogue | GET /api/marketdata/connectivity | web/src/api/client.ts | ores.marketdata | seven collection reads in parallel, each parsed and sorted by name | ETag on the whole view, stale after 5 min |
| the cuts | GET /api/reporting/cuts, GET /api/reporting/cuts/:id, GET /api/reporting/cuts/:id/runs | web/src/api/client.ts | ores.reporting | the cuts of the session's party with the derived state, label and run tally the reporting service answers, or one cut with its kind and the gaps the assembly could not fill | ETag on the whole view, stale after 15 s |
| the deployment overview | GET /api/overview | web/src/api/client.ts | ores.iam | the tenant roster counted by status, its first page, and the newest setups, each count a separate list read | ETag on the whole view, stale after 15 s |
| the grid series | GET /api/operations/grid/series | web/src/api/client.ts | ores.compute | the counters per bucket joined to one row of buckets per node and the host registry, two reads | ETag on the whole view, stale after 10 s |
| the grid summary | GET /api/operations/grid | web/src/api/client.ts | ores.compute | the stored summary, the host registry with its hardware rows, and the service roster, three reads | ETag on the whole view, stale after 10 s |
| the grid usage | GET /api/operations/grid/usage | web/src/api/client.ts | ores.compute | the results grouped by tenant or by party with each group's share, and the party names beside them | ETag on the whole view, stale after 30 s |
| the hand take | POST /api/reporting/cuts/take | web/src/api/client.ts | ores.reporting | the body assembled from the trading, market data, compute and reporting reads, answered part by part with the gaps it could not fill, and the cut the press wrote with the approval request it raised | no cache: the call writes when it does not preview |
| the image map | GET /api/image-map | web/src/api/client.ts | ores.assets | which image every flagged record uses: currencies, countries, calendars and business centres in one code to image map, five reads, with a country fallback and a placeholder | ETag on the whole view, stale after 5 min |
| the kinds of cut | DELETE /api/reporting/cut-kinds/:code, GET /api/reporting/cut-kinds, GET /api/reporting/cut-kinds/:code, PUT /api/reporting/cut-kinds/:code | web/src/api/client.ts | ores.reporting | the cut kinds with their family and resolution rule names, the approval kinds the kinds point at, and one cut read for the counts, four reads | ETag on the whole view, stale after 60 s |
| the lookups a cut kind names | GET /api/reporting/cut-kind-choices | web/src/api/client.ts | ores.reporting | the cut families, the cut resolution rules and the inbox approval kinds, three collection reads | ETag on the whole view, stale after 5 min |
| the market data catalogue | GET /api/marketdata/catalogue | web/src/api/client.ts | ores.marketdata | the providers and the feeds that serve them, two collection reads | ETag on the whole view, stale after 5 min |
| the market trace | GET /api/synthetic/market-trace | web/src/api/client.ts | ores.synthetic | the scheduler's recent decisions: every tick with the gap its law drew and the wall gap it took, every regime turn and pin, and every change of the feeds | ETag on the whole view, stale after 10 s |
| the message bus | GET /api/operations/bus | web/src/api/client.ts | ores.telemetry | the NATS server samples over a window, and one read per stream because no operation lists the streams | ETag on the whole view, stale after 10 s |
| the principal's ways to hold | GET /api/me/ways-to-hold | web/src/api/client.ts | ores.iam | the role catalogue and what each role grants against the grants the person already holds | ETag on the whole view, stale after 5 min |
| the public publishers | GET /api/marketdata/public-rates | web/src/api/client.ts | ores.marketdata | the providers, feeds, scopes, sessions, suppliers and market series read together and joined onto each publisher | ETag on the whole view, stale after 5 min |
| the public rates coverage | GET /api/marketdata/public-rates/coverage | web/src/api/client.ts | ores.marketdata | the public catalogue and its fixings, reduced to each series' first date, last date and count | ETag on the whole view, stale after 5 min |
| the public rates file | GET /api/marketdata/public-rates/fixings.csv | web/src/api/client.ts | ores.marketdata | the public catalogue to find the publisher's series, then their fixings filtered to the range and item | no cache: a file is built per request |
| the report health | GET /api/reporting/report-health | web/src/api/client.ts | ores.reporting | each report definition with the row the list would draw and the reads that failed, so a failed read is not read as never having run | ETag on the whole view, stale after 60 s |
| the request checks | GET /api/requests/:id/checks | web/src/api/client.ts | ores.workflow | each proposed line run through the real write against the record as it stands | ETag on the whole view, stale after 30 s |
| the request story | GET /api/requests/:id/changes, GET /api/requests/:id/story | web/src/api/client.ts | ores.workflow | the request's versions, the decisions taken on it, the roles it asked for and the notices, or the change lines of its kind beside the live record | ETag on the whole view, stale after 15 s |
| the run's progress | GET /api/workflow/:instanceId | web/src/api/client.ts | ores.workflow | the run's status, the step executing and one summary per step | ETag on the whole view, stale after 5 s |
| the sprint board | GET /api/agile/board | web/src/api/agile.ts | no service, X1 decides | one sprint's work items and their states, read from the sprint file and the documents under it | ETag on the whole view, stale after 60 s |
| the sprint charts | GET /api/agile/charts | web/src/api/agile.ts | no service, X1 decides | one sprint's scope and the work remaining per day, computed from the sprint file and the state changes under it | ETag on the whole view, stale after 60 s |
| the synthetic folder tree | GET /api/synthetic/feed-folders | web/src/api/client.ts | ores.synthetic | the folders and the feeds under each, at any depth, with the count of each kind so no screen reads the feeds | ETag on the whole view, stale after 60 s |
| the tenor catalogue | GET /api/tenors/catalogue | web/src/api/tenors.ts | ores.refdata | the tenors with their conventions and schedules, three collection reads | ETag on the whole view, stale after 5 min |

* The coverage

How many BFF routes each area pairs with a C++ route, counted from
=build/scripts/route_pairing.py=. A pair records the mapping and does not
prove it; the port tasks prove each route against the answer the BFF gives.

| Area | BFF routes | Paired | Gaps |
|------+------------+--------+------|
| access | 31 | 31 | 0 |
| agile | 6 | 0 | 6 |
| analytics | 6 | 0 | 6 |
| assets | 5 | 4 | 1 |
| auth | 18 | 15 | 3 |
| changes | 2 | 2 | 0 |
| grid | 14 | 0 | 14 |
| market-data | 31 | 0 | 31 |
| operations | 5 | 1 | 4 |
| qa | 6 | 0 | 6 |
| reference-data | 30 | 1 | 29 |
| reporting | 44 | 0 | 44 |
| simulation | 28 | 0 | 28 |
| tenancy | 21 | 9 | 12 |
| trading | 24 | 0 | 24 |
| workflow | 18 | 0 | 18 |
| (none) | 35 | 12 | 23 |
| total | 324 | 75 | 249 |
